# Reel Places

**Locations saved from Instagram reels, divided by region and state — with a map, a pin for every place, what each reel recommended it for, and a link back to the source reel.**

## How to use this list

- 🧭 Organized **Region → Park or City → State → Activity → Trail or Site** — e.g. Southwest → Zion National Park → Utah → Sunrise → Canyon Overlook. Counts: Northeast 51 · Southeast 50 · Midwest 45 · Southwest 73 · Mountain West 57 · West 55 · Pacific Northwest 21 · Alaska 29 · Hawaii 7 · U.S. Territories 3 · **Total 391**
- 📍 Every place name links to its Google Maps location. Each entry also shows what the reel recommended it for and links to that reel.
- 🎬 **Source reel** links go back to the original Instagram reel, so you can re-watch why it was saved.
- 💬 **Ask the chatbot** — [Reel Chat](chat.html) is the project's one chatbot (the Streamlit app — that page takes you straight to it): it answers questions and builds itineraries from these recommendations.

### Acadia National Park

#### Maine

##### Hike

- **1. [Precipice Trail](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Precipice+Trail+Acadia+National+Park+ME)** — Acadia National Park, Acadia National Park
  - **Best for:** Hike / Adrenaline — His #1 adrenaline hike: climbers ascending near-vertical granite using iron rungs and ladders, with coastal ocean views from the summit. Across the countdown he frames the five as thrilling but accessible adventures, describing each hike's challenge, exposure and reward.
  - 🥾 AllTrails: [Trail page](https://www.alltrails.com/trail/us/maine/precipice-loop)
  - Pin is the trailhead point on the Park Loop Road (OSM's 'The Precipice Trail' information point).
  - 🎬 Source: [Top 5 National Park Adrenaline Hikes](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DeRqnZahkTx/) by @amorywild (#1 in that reel)
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Have you done this hike?? Precipice Trail, Acadia](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfaOC7PRf92/) by @daveandjoce
    - **Best for:** Hike / Adrenaline · Iron-rung cliff climb in Acadia — A second take on Acadia's Precipice Trail: Dave & Joce ask 'Have you done this hike?? What'd you think' — the near-vertical iron-rung and ladder climb up Champlain Mountain's east face, famous as one of the most thrilling (and exposed) trails in the eastern national parks.

##### Sunrise

- **2. [Beehive Trail](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Beehive+Trail+Acadia+National+Park+ME)** — Acadia National Park, Acadia National Park
  - **Best for:** Sunrise / Summit view / Hike — Caption names Acadia's Beehive Trail at sunrise. After finishing their 63rd national park, the creators film themselves squinting into the sun, then pan across the rocky granite summit to a forested coastline, islands, curving shoreline, and golden reflection on the ocean.
  - 🥾 AllTrails: [Trail page](https://www.alltrails.com/trail/us/maine/the-beehive-loop-trail)
  - 🎬 Source: [Hiking / Sunrise — Beehive Trail](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DdJgrUhvccf/) by @amorywild (#None in that reel)
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Acadia's iconic sunrises — Cadillac Mountain & the Beehive](https://www.instagram.com/reel/De3GgUCgfu4/) by @daveandjoce
    - **Best for:** Sunrise — Dave & Joce's favorite Acadia sunrise spot — 'another unreal sunrise spot' besides Cadillac Mountain: the Beehive Trail, the park's famous iron-rung climb above Sand Beach.
- **3. [Cadillac Mountain](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cadillac+Mountain+Acadia+National+Park+ME)** — Acadia National Park, Bar Harbor
  - **Best for:** Sunrise — Dave & Joce's pick for one of the most iconic sunrises in the U.S.: Cadillac Mountain, famous for being one of the first places in the country to see the sunrise during parts of the year. If you're driving up, vehicle reservations are required during the reservation season — or you can hike to the top.
  - Coordinates verified against OpenStreetMap.
  - 🎬 Source: [Acadia's iconic sunrises — Cadillac Mountain & the Beehive](https://www.instagram.com/reel/De3GgUCgfu4/) by @daveandjoce (#1 in that reel)

##### Top national park (East)

- **4. [Acadia National Park](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Acadia+National+Park+ME)** — Acadia National Park, Bar Harbor
  - **Best for:** Top national park (East) — Ranked #5 in the East, shown with mountain, lake and coastline views at sunrise/sunset, iron-rung climbs, a loon and seals on a sunset boat cruise.
  - 🎬 Source: [My Top 5 National Parks in the EAST](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DenDeSLBwwP/) by @amorywild (#5 in that reel)
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Wildlife — Acadia National Park](https://www.instagram.com/reel/Ddt8odbRZ32/) by @amorywild
    - **Best for:** Beaver / Wildlife — Caption names Acadia for wildlife. A beaver swims through a lily-pad-covered pond carrying plant material, pauses to eat, and continues slowly through the water; on-screen text reads, 'The pure joy this brings me...'
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Wildlife / Sunset — Acadia National Park](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DdMLqCPOics/) by @amorywild
    - **Best for:** Seals / Sunset / Wildlife — Caption names Acadia at sunset for wildlife. From a boat, the camera zooms toward a rocky outcrop where harbor seals are hauled out and swimming nearby as orange and pink light reflects on calm water; on-screen text reads, 'POV you're watching seals at sunset in Acadia...'
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Hiking — Acadia National Park](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DdILP0qJ6Op/) by @amorywild
    - **Best for:** Iron-rung hike / View — Caption complains, 'So many ladders...' on an Acadia hike. The POV video climbs installed metal rungs and ladders on a near-vertical granite face, crosses an exposed ledge, and reaches a summit view over green mountains and coastal islands; the specific trail is not named by the creator.
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Acadia's iconic sunrises — Cadillac Mountain & the Beehive](https://www.instagram.com/reel/De3GgUCgfu4/) by @daveandjoce
    - **Best for:** Sunrise — Dave & Joce came to Acadia for one of the most iconic sunrises in the U.S.: Cadillac Mountain (first sunrise in the country for part of the year; vehicle reservations required in season, or hike up) — and their personal favorite, the Beehive Trail. 'Send this to the people you're waking up at 3am with.'

### Acadia National Park (Sieur de Monts)

#### Maine

##### Hike · Short historic stone-step path to Dorr Mountain

- **5. [Homans Path](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Homans+Path+Acadia)** — Acadia National Park (Sieur de Monts), Bar Harbor
  - **Best for:** Hike · Short historic stone-step path to Dorr Mountain — One of the shortest hikes in Acadia National Park might also be one of the most unique: tucked into the Sieur de Monts area near Bar Harbor, Homans Path is just 0.3 miles one way (0.8 mi out-and-back with ~334 ft of climb) but packs in intricate granite staircases, narrow forest passages, historic stonework, a stone 'hallway' and beautiful views on a short but steep climb. The reel pairs it into a half-day Sieur de Monts itinerary: Wild Gardens of Acadia, the Nature Center, the Jesup Path boardwalk, then Homans Path, with the Island Explorer shuttle as an option. The path is a memorial trail honoring Eliza Lothrop Homans' land donations; it joins the Schiff Path toward the summit of Dorr Mountain.
  - 🥾 AllTrails: [Trail page](https://www.alltrails.com/trail/us/maine/homans-path--2)
  - Pin is Sieur de Monts Spring — the trail begins at Sieur de Monts, steps from the spring and the Wild Gardens of Acadia.
  - 🎬 Source: [One of the shortest hikes in Acadia might be the most unique: Homans Path](https://www.instagram.com/reel/Dd0ePLKxuJF/) by @the_roamsteaders (#None in that reel)

### Cherry Springs State Park

#### Pennsylvania

##### Stargazing

- **13. [Cherry Springs State Park](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cherry+Springs+State+Park+PA)** — Cherry Springs State Park, Coudersport
  - **Best for:** Stargazing — Ranked #5 in Adventure Family Vlog's '10 Places in America Where the Night Sky Doesn't Look Real' — picked as one of America's most incredible dark-sky destinations for stargazing and Milky Way photography.
  - Park-level entry from the dark-sky carousel, which names the place as a whole rather than a specific viewpoint. Coordinates verified against OpenStreetMap.
  - 🎬 Source: [10 Places in America Where the Night Sky Doesn't Look Real! (+ 5 bonus)](https://www.instagram.com/p/DfK2OLUiTVM/) by @adventurefamilyvlog (#5 in that reel)
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Pennsylvania Road Trip Ideas Part 2](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfU-tMFRe-v/) by @hunterhulbert
    - **Best for:** Road trip stop — Honorable mention in Hunter Hulbert's Pennsylvania road trip reel — one of the PA Wilds stops he says to add to the route; the park's dark skies are its fame.
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [You haven't really seen the night sky until you've visited places like these](https://www.instagram.com/p/DfD2yGrmvkK/) by @earthpulse111
    - **Best for:** Stargazing — The carousel's Pennsylvania pick: noted as one of the best places to see stars in the eastern United States.
- **14. [Overnight Astronomy Observation Field](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cherry+Springs+State+Park+Astronomy+Observation+Field+PA)** — Cherry Springs State Park, Coudersport
  - **Best for:** Stargazing — Visit Dark Skies PA's pick for the best stargazing setup at Cherry Springs: the Overnight Astronomy Observation Field, with concrete telescope pads, electrical outlets and WiFi. The rules that keep it world-class: a permit/registration is required, arrive and set up before dark (vehicles then stay in the adjacent lot), red lights only — no white light — no campfires, and quiet hours from midnight to 9 AM.
  - Pin shares the Cherry Springs State Park coordinates; the observation field is inside the park.
  - 🎬 Source: [Overnight Astronomy Observation Field — Cherry Springs State Park](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DdHyZ9qRHJZ/) by @visitdarkskiespa (#1 in that reel)

### Green Lakes State Park

#### New York

##### Meromictic lakes · Blue-green water without a major hike

- **22. [Green Lakes State Park](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Green+Lakes+State+Park+NY)** — Green Lakes State Park, Fayetteville
  - **Best for:** Meromictic lakes · Blue-green water without a major hike — Two rare meromictic lakes — Green Lake and Round Lake — whose deep waters never mix seasonally, and whose unusual chemistry creates a striking blue-green color that looks like it should need a passport. Walk the easy shoreline trail, swim at the beach in season, kayak, or picnic — a few hours of exploring without needing a major hike.
  - 🎬 Source: [I thought this shade of blue required a passport](https://www.instagram.com/reel/Depe-uMJ5j8/) by @daytripnomad (#None in that reel)

### Kinzua Bridge State Park

#### Pennsylvania

##### Scenic overlook · Skywalk on the collapsed Kinzua Viaduct

- **26. [Kinzua Bridge State Park](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Kinzua+Bridge+State+Park+PA)** — Kinzua Bridge State Park, Mount Jewett
  - **Best for:** Scenic overlook · Skywalk on the collapsed Kinzua Viaduct — Honorable mention in the reel's caption: the Kinzua Viaduct in McKean County — once among the longest and highest railroad bridges in the world, toppled by a tornado in 2003 and reinvented as a skywalk out over the gorge, with the twisted towers still lying in the valley below.
  - 🥾 AllTrails: [Trail page](https://www.alltrails.com/trail/us/pennsylvania/kinzua-bridge-trail)
  - The skywalk has been under intermittent repair closures scheduled through 2027, with seasonal openings — check the park page before going.
  - 🎬 Source: [Pennsylvania Road Trip Ideas Part 2](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfU-tMFRe-v/) by @hunterhulbert (#None in that reel)

### Leonard Harrison State Park

#### Pennsylvania

##### Scenic overlook · PA Grand Canyon overlooks and Turkey Path waterfall

- **27. [Pine Creek Gorge (PA Grand Canyon)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Pine+Creek+Gorge+Leonard+Harrison+State+Park+PA)** — Leonard Harrison State Park, Wellsboro
  - **Best for:** Scenic overlook · PA Grand Canyon overlooks and Turkey Path waterfall — Tioga County's 'Grand Canyon of Pennsylvania' — a roughly 45-mile-long, 1,000-foot-deep forested gorge. The reel shows the rim overlooks at Leonard Harrison State Park, hikers on the cliff edges, and the Turkey Path trail dropping past its waterfall toward Pine Creek.

### Monadnock State Park

#### New Hampshire

##### Hike

- **31. [Mount Monadnock](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Mount+Monadnock+New+Hampshire)** — Monadnock State Park, Jaffrey
  - **Best for:** Hike / Summit · Bare rocky summit with 360° views — Calvin's reel takes on Mt. Monadnock in southern New Hampshire — the 3,165-ft peak that gave 'monadnock' its name (an isolated mountain rising from the plain), reputed to be one of the most-climbed mountains in the world. The classic route is the White Dot Trail up from the state park headquarters (3.4–3.6 mi round trip, ~1,750 ft gain, with rock scrambles and stone steps) and down the White Cross — the bare, rocky summit opens to sweeping views across southern New Hampshire and, on clear days, all six New England states. A National Natural Landmark with a literary streak: Emerson and Thoreau both climbed and wrote about it. No dogs allowed on the mountain.
  - 🥾 AllTrails: [Trail page](https://www.alltrails.com/trail/us/new-hampshire/white-dot-to-mount-monadnock)
  - 🎬 Source: [Mt. Monadnock in New Hampshire](https://www.instagram.com/reel/De93H7Iyu6a/) by @calvin_on_the_internet (#None in that reel)

### Buffalo National River (Ponca Wilderness)

#### Arkansas

##### Hike · Narrow ledge trail on a 550-ft bluff over the Buffalo River

- **4. [Goat Trail to Big Bluff](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Centerpoint+Trailhead+Ponca+Arkansas)** — Buffalo National River (Ponca Wilderness), Ponca
  - **Best for:** Hike · Narrow ledge trail on a 550-ft bluff over the Buffalo River — A strenuous 6-mile round trip in the Ponca Wilderness: follow the Centerpoint Trail down from Highway 43, then take the Goat Trail spur — a narrow ledge carved across the face of Big Bluff, at 550 feet one of the tallest sheer bluffs between the Rockies and the Appalachians — for the huge view over the Buffalo River. Rugged, rocky, and a real climb back out; the reel's trail warning is plenty of horse poop along the way. Not for kids or anyone afraid of heights.
  - 🥾 AllTrails: [Trail page](https://www.alltrails.com/trail/us/arkansas/goat-trail-to-big-bluff)
  - Pin is the Centerpoint Trailhead on AR-43 north of Ponca (the hike's start); Big Bluff is about 3 trail-miles in.
  - 🎬 Source: [You hiked 6 miles to see THIS Arkansas view](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfM53guRj7X/) by @heyarkansas (#None in that reel)

##### Waterfall

- **5. [Hemmed-In Hollow Falls](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Hemmed-in-Hollow+Falls+Arkansas)** — Buffalo National River (Ponca Wilderness), Compton
  - **Best for:** Waterfall / Hike · Tallest waterfall between the Rockies and Appalachians — A nearly 210-foot waterfall hidden in the Ponca Wilderness of Buffalo National River — the tallest waterfall between the Rocky and Appalachian Mountains. The journey is part of it: a steep, rugged hike drops nearly 1,400 feet into the hollow, and you climb every bit of it back out. Key catch from the reel: it's rain-fed, so it can be a powerful cascade after significant rainfall or barely a trickle in dry periods — go after rain (late winter through spring is peak flow).
  - 🥾 AllTrails: [Trail page](https://www.alltrails.com/trail/us/arkansas/hemmed-in-hollow-trail)
  - 🎬 Source: [Arkansas is keeping too many secrets — a nearly 210-foot waterfall](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DczeYNly63D/) by @heyarkansas (#None in that reel)

### Rainbow Springs State Park

#### Florida

##### Spring swimming · Kayak a crystal-clear 72°F river

- **39. [Rainbow Springs State Park](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Rainbow+Springs+State+Park+FL)** — Rainbow Springs State Park, Dunnellon
  - **Best for:** Spring swimming · Kayak a crystal-clear 72°F river — One of the oldest and most beautiful spring-fed rivers in Florida: crystal-clear water at 72°F year-round, surrounded by lush greenery that feels like a tropical jungle. It's Florida's 4th-largest spring; the headspring area was a privately owned theme park in the 1930s. Kayak, paddleboard, swim in the designated area, or walk the garden trails to see waterfalls — real ones, in Florida. Open daily 8 AM to sunset, $2 entry (tubing and kayak rentals extra). Pro tip from the reel: come early morning or late afternoon to beat the crowds, and bring your own snorkel gear to spot fish and turtles.
  - 🎬 Source: [Rainbow Springs — Florida's crystal-clear spring river](https://www.instagram.com/reel/Dc7ZhgwRA9P/) by @americantravelarchives (#None in that reel)

Year-round 60°F underground, and heavy rain or snow actually increases the flow. Adult tickets $28.95 per the reel; most people spend 2–3 hours (the tour itself ~90 minutes).

### Custer State Park (Needles Highway)

#### South Dakota

##### Scenic drive · One-lane tunnel carved through granite

- **16. [Needles Eye Tunnel](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Needles+Eye+Tunnel+South+Dakota)** — Custer State Park (Needles Highway), Custer
  - **Best for:** Scenic drive · One-lane tunnel carved through granite — The Needles Highway through Custer State Park winds between towering granite spires until the road tightens and passes through the Needles Eye Tunnel — a one-lane tunnel hand-carved through solid granite in the 1920s, one of the most photographed drives in the Black Hills. Know before you go: the tunnel is about 8'4" wide and 12' tall, one vehicle at a time, and RVs/trailers should avoid the route; drive it early morning or late afternoon for fewer cars and better light, and watch for bison, pronghorn and prairie dogs nearby.
  - 🎬 Source: [This state park drive feels unreal the first time you see it](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfW_ZwsAprR/) by @coupleofpatels (#None in that reel)

- **20. [Johnson's Shut-Ins State Park](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Johnson%27s+Shut-Ins+State+Park+Missouri)** — Johnson's Shut-Ins State Park, Middle Brook
  - **Best for:** Swimming — One of the most unique swims in the Midwest: hundreds of natural pools and chutes the Black River carved into billion-year-old volcanic rock — 'nature's own waterpark,' about 2 hours south of St. Louis. The reel's tips: go early in summer (it gets busy), wear water shoes for the slippery rock, and mind the stronger currents with kids.
  - Pin sits at the main shut-ins day-use area (OpenStreetMap point for the swimming area's supply store) rather than the park boundary centroid. The 'shut-ins' are narrow channels where the river squeezes through erosion-resistant rhyolite.
  - 🎬 Source: [Johnson's Shut-Ins State Park — nature's waterpark in Missouri](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfUarsMgzMy/) by @coupleofpatels (#None in that reel)

### Peninsula State Park

#### Wisconsin

##### Fall colors

- **26. [Eagle Tower](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Eagle+Tower+Peninsula+State+Park+Fish+Creek+WI)** — Peninsula State Park, Fish Creek
  - **Best for:** Fall colors / View — The reel's headline stop: add Peninsula State Park in Door County to a Wisconsin trip for Eagle Tower, reached by a long wooden elevated walkway through autumn trees to panoramic views over Green Bay and the surrounding islands — at their best in fall color. Parking, restrooms and park information are right at the tower area.
  - The reel says the views overlook Lake Michigan; the tower actually faces Green Bay (the bay between the Door Peninsula and mainland Wisconsin), with Horseshoe Island in view. Caption puts the park about a 5-hour drive from Chicago. The rebuilt tower (opened 2021) is reached by 95-100 stairs or an 850-foot accessible canopy ramp; Wisconsin DNR lists it open May 1-Oct 31.

### Pinnacles Youth Park

#### Missouri

##### Hike · Towering rock pinnacles and fossil hunting

- **31. [Pinnacles Youth Park](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Pinnacles+Youth+Park+Missouri)** — Pinnacles Youth Park, Sturgeon
  - **Best for:** Hike · Towering rock pinnacles and fossil hunting — One of the most unique places in Missouri: a privately owned park north of Columbia (850 E. Pinnacles Rd, Sturgeon) that's open for public day use and feels like a rugged hike out west. Lower trails run along the creek to a shelter bluff for all ages; the real adventure is crossing the creek and taking the narrow, rocky path over the top of the towering rock formations. The spot is full of fossils — look in the creek and across the rocks for thousands of crinoids. Free (donation box at the kiosk); vault restroom; no alcohol, glass or drones. The reel's warnings: the upper ledge trails are not for young kids or anyone afraid of heights, don't cross the creek when the water is high, and expect wet feet even in summer.
  - 🎬 Source: [This is Missouri?! Pinnacles Youth Park](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfYsfYBOaqy/) by @midwestnomadfamily (#None in that reel)
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Plan a Missouri fall adventure: Columbia + Rocheport day trip](https://www.instagram.com/reel/De29PU7Oj9j/) by @midwestnomadfamily
    - **Best for:** Hike · Towering rock pinnacles and fossil hunting — Also a stop on this Columbia + Rocheport fall day trip: a hidden gem that's private property but open to the public — lower trails to a shelter cave and creek for all ages, while older kids and adults can carefully climb along the rock Pinnacles. Note from this reel: no dogs, and the restrooms are primitive.

### Bentonite Hills (Cow Dung Road, near the Mars Desert Research Station)

#### Utah

##### Viewpoint

- **4. [Bentonite Hills](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Bentonite+Hills+UT)** — Bentonite Hills (Cow Dung Road, near the Mars Desert Research Station), Hanksville
  - **Best for:** Viewpoint — Ranked #4 in Adventure Family Vlog's '9 Places in Utah That Look Unreal — and none are in a national park': rainbow-colored hills that look like Mars, in the badlands west of Hanksville.
  - Two Bentonite Hills areas exist: the official ones inside Capitol Reef (Cathedral Valley) and the photographed BLM hills on Cow Dung Road near the Mars Desert Research Station outside Hanksville. Both source reels point to the Hanksville/MDRS hills (the 'none in a national park' list rules out the park site), so the pin uses the Cow Dung Road coordinates given by several independent guides (~38.4123, -110.7859).
  - 🎬 Source: [9 Places in Utah That Look Unreal — None in a National Park](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfKQdjWx1L3/) by @adventurefamilyvlog (#4 in that reel)
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Bentonite Hills — a landscape from another planet](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfP6OiPPW-W/) by @megsandpat
    - **Best for:** Viewpoint — Megs and Pat's Mars-on-Earth pick: hills of bentonite clay near Hanksville, just outside Capitol Reef, striped purple, pink, red, orange, grey and blue-green from volcanic ash and minerals. Their practical notes: on the road toward the Mars Desert Research Station; unpaved access, high-clearance 4WD recommended; best at sunrise or sunset when low light brings out the colours; and never after rain — wet bentonite turns extremely slippery and sticky and vehicles get stuck.

### Hell Hole Canyon

#### Utah

##### Waterfall

- **41. [Kayenta Falls](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Kayenta+Falls+Ivins+UT)** — Hell Hole Canyon, Ivins
  - **Best for:** Waterfall — One of Southern Utah's rarest sights, per Mikey Loveridge: the 1,000-foot red-rock cliffs of Hell Hole Canyon near Ivins run bone dry most days, but after heavy rain three ephemeral waterfalls — Kayenta Falls — pour straight down the cliff faces in a vertical flash flood. He photographed the canyon completely dry on Nov 1, 2026, and in full flow the next day after the rain kept coming.
  - 🥾 AllTrails: [Trail page](https://www.alltrails.com/trail/us/utah/hellhole-trail)
  - Pin is approximate: the ephemeral falls are not a mapped feature, so it sits on Kayenta Wash at the mouth of Hell Hole Canyon, just northwest of the Kayenta community in Ivins (OpenStreetMap). The falls flow only after heavy rain.
  - 🎬 Source: [Kayenta Falls — A Vertical Flash Flood](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfQEa3Ph55r/) by @mikeyloveridge (#1 in that reel)

  - **Best for:** Sunrise — Ranked #3 for sunrise in the reel. Massive red rock cliffs glow bright orange as the sun hits Zion Canyon.

Incredible at sunrise or sunset. Short but with exposed drop-offs, so keep kids close.

A short hike that delivers big views, worth adding to a Zion itinerary. Parking is very limited, so arrive early.

### Dinosaur National Monument

#### Utah

##### Fossils · A building built around a wall of dinosaur bones

- **3. [Quarry Exhibit Hall](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Quarry+Exhibit+Hall+Dinosaur+National+Monument)** — Dinosaur National Monument, Jensen
  - **Best for:** Fossils · A building built around a wall of dinosaur bones — About 30 minutes east of Vernal, a shuttle takes you to the Quarry Exhibit Hall — a building constructed around the sloped rock face where a 'log jam' of dinosaur bones was found in 1909. Hundreds of thousands of bones were excavated for museums around the world, but the rest were left in place, so you can see — and touch — real fossils where they were buried. You can shuttle back or hike back down for more fossils in the rock formations. The monument spans Utah and Colorado; the quarry is on the Utah side, and the creators wished they'd had time for more trails and a Green River float.
  - 🎬 Source: [Finding, seeing, touching dinosaur fossils at Dinosaur National Monument](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DdglrAsBsTg/) by @the_vg_odyssey (#None in that reel)

### East Zion (Orderville)

#### Utah

##### Slot canyon

- **4. [Crimson Canyon](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Crimson+Canyon+East+Zion+Adventures+Orderville)** — East Zion (Orderville), Orderville
  - **Best for:** Slot canyon / Guided tour · Antelope-style canyon without the crowds — The reel's pitch: people pay hundreds to visit Antelope Canyon, but almost no one knows about this slot canyon near Zion — towering red sandstone walls (100+ ft high, 6–10 ft wide), winding passageways and incredible photo opportunities WITHOUT the crowds. The catch, stated honestly in the reel: Crimson Canyon itself has no entrance fee, but you can't access it independently because the route crosses private property — you book a guided tour with a company that has permission. They booked East Zion Adventures and loved it: a ~2-hour trip, UTV ride into the backcountry from Orderville, then a guided hike with short climbs (obstacles up to ~10 ft).
  - Pin is the tour meeting point in Orderville (15 E State St — check-in at the Sinclair station coffee shop, per East Zion Adventures' listings); the canyon itself is on private land reached by UTV and has no public coordinates.
  - 🎬 Source: [People pay hundreds for Antelope Canyon — almost no one knows this slot canyon near Zion](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfbItoFgJHj/) by @adventurefamilyvlog (#None in that reel)

### Glacier National Park (Many Glacier)

### Homestead Resort

#### Utah

##### Hot spring · Swim inside a 55-foot limestone crater

- **19. [Homestead Crater](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Homestead+Crater+Midway+UT)** — Homestead Resort, Midway
  - **Best for:** Hot spring · Swim inside a 55-foot limestone crater — A hidden underground hot spring inside a 55-foot-tall limestone crater: a naturally heated geothermal pool at 90–96°F year-round, about 65 feet deep, that you can soak, swim, snorkel or even scuba dive in. It's at the Homestead Resort in Midway, just outside Park City, but day visitors are welcome — $30 per person (the reel notes it was $15 when filmed) for a 60-minute session with about 40 minutes in the water; life jackets are required and provided, and reservations are recommended since weekends fill up.
  - 🎬 Source: [There's a hidden underground hot spring in Utah… and you can swim inside it!](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DecRg0zxbPF/) by @adventurefamilyvlog (#None in that reel)

Their plan: stay at Morefield Campground as the home base inside the park, start at the Visitor & Research Center to learn the history and plan the visit, book ranger-guided tours through Recreation.gov to get up close to the dwellings (they loved Cliff Palace and Balcony House), and grab fry bread at Soda Point after a day of exploring.

### Million Dollar Highway (Ouray to Silverton)

#### Colorado

##### Scenic drive

- **26. [Million Dollar Highway (US-550)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Million+Dollar+Highway+Colorado)** — Million Dollar Highway (Ouray to Silverton), Ouray
  - **Best for:** Scenic drive — American Travel Archives' 'you will NOT believe this road' drive: the Million Dollar Highway (US-550) from Ouray to Silverton — 25 miles through the San Juan Mountains famous for steep cliffs, tight switchbacks and few guardrails. Stop at Red Mountain Pass for the classic overlook; autumn is the best time, when golden aspens contrast with the red and gray rock. Their tips: drive it in good morning light, take your time at the pull-offs, and think twice in an RV. Ouray and Silverton bookend it with hot springs and silver-mining history.
  - Pin is at Red Mountain Pass, the highway's classic overlook (OpenStreetMap).
  - 🎬 Source: [Million Dollar Highway (US-550) — Ouray to Silverton, CO](https://www.instagram.com/reel/Dccc6pnxZSm/) by @americantravelarchives (#1 in that reel)

  - **Best for:** Dinosaur tracks · Fossil tracksite a short walk from parking — Stop #2 on the reel's Highway 279 drive: the Poison Spider tracksite — real three-toed Jurassic dinosaur tracks on rock slabs, a short walk from the trailhead parking; viewing tubes in the BLM area line up on a series of fossilized tracks and a petroglyph panel.
  - Pin at the Poison Spider trailhead parking, byway mile ~5.9 (milepoint-anchored to the Corona Arch trailhead OSM point).

  - **Best for:** Hike / Arch · Massive freestanding arch, ladder + cable section — Stop #4 on the reel's Highway 279 drive: Corona Arch — a massive freestanding arch (140 by 105 ft, also called Little Rainbow Bridge) on a 3-mile round-trip hike with a short ladder and cable section; the trail also passes Bowtie Arch.
  - Pin is the Corona Arch Trailhead (the OSM-listed trailhead point); the arch is at the end of the ~1.5-mile trail.

  - **Best for:** Rock art · Ancient petroglyphs at a marked roadside pullout — Stop #1 on the reel's Highway 279 drive: an ancient petroglyph panel right along the road, with a marked pullout so you can see the rock art without hiking — dozens of carvings (bighorn sheep, hunters, spirals) on the varnished cliff, with BLM interpretive signs.

The video zooms in on a marmot nestled in a crevice between boulders, then pans to reveal its 'view': a tall, powerful waterfall cascading down a sheer cliff into a calm pool, snow patches near the top. The waterfall is not named anywhere in the post, so this entry is at park level.
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Wildlife — Yellowstone National Park](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DdnMCCbN6CM/) by @amorywild
    - **Best for:** Bison calves / Wildlife — Caption names Yellowstone for wildlife. An adult bison grazes in a grassland while a small reddish calf takes tentative steps nearby; on-screen text reads, 'Bring me back to red dog season...', referring to newborn bison calves' red coats.
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Top 10 National Parks](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DdaWOp1N9L3/) by @amorywild
    - **Best for:** Top national park — His #1 national park: Yellowstone, shown with bison including a calf, grizzly bears in meadows, and Lower Falls with a rainbow.
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Wyoming bucket-list trip — Grand Teton & Yellowstone in 5 days](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfQimY6Pasm/) by @roamingwithtk
    - **Best for:** Itinerary — Days 2–5 of Taylor & Kristen's 5-day Wyoming trip (only 3 days of PTO): Old Faithful and the Grand Prismatic Overlook hike, an early-morning Lamar Valley drive for wolves and bison, the Grand Canyon of the Yellowstone from Artist Point, Hayden Valley at sunset, the Upper Geyser Basin, Yellowstone Lake, Great Fountain Geyser at sunset, and a bonus day of Mystic Falls or Mount Washburn plus Mammoth Hot Springs.

### Devils Postpile National Monument

### Giant Sequoia National Monument

#### California

##### Waterfall

- **16. [Seven Teacups](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Seven+Teacups+Kern+River+California)** — Giant Sequoia National Monument, Kernville
  - **Best for:** Waterfall — A surreal chain of smooth white-granite pools on Dry Meadow Creek, each cup dropping 6 to 20 feet into the next before the water slides into the Kern River. The creator's warning, kept verbatim in spirit: please don't swim — the current can pull swimmers under even when the water looks calm.

The reel names the AllTrails route 'Seven Tea Cups Viewpoint via Dry Creek Meadow' and mentions Johnny McNally's Fairview Lodge-Restaurant, about 10 minutes away, as a nearby stop (an amenity mention, not pinned).

### Julia Pfeiffer Burns State Park

#### California

##### Hike · Redwood canyon loop to coastal ridge views

- **21. [Ewoldsen Trail](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Ewoldsen+Trail+Julia+Pfeiffer+Burns+State+Park+CA)** — Julia Pfeiffer Burns State Park, Big Sur
  - **Best for:** Hike · Redwood canyon loop to coastal ridge views — One of the four hikes the reel names under its #4 Big Sur pick: the loop up McWay Creek through redwood forest to open coastal ridge views — often called the best day hike in Big Sur.

  - Pin is the Ewoldsen Trailhead at the park's main parking area. AllTrails listed the trail as closed in fall 2026 due to fire damage — check current conditions before going.

  - **Best for:** Hike · Short trail through a rock tunnel to a hidden cove — One of the four spots the reel names under its #4 Big Sur pick: a short, steep trail down through a hand-carved tunnel to a turquoise cove once used to load tanbark onto ships.

### McArthur-Burney Falls Memorial State Park

#### California

##### Waterfall · 129-ft spring-fed falls flowing year-round

- **30. [Burney Falls](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Burney+Falls+California)** — McArthur-Burney Falls Memorial State Park, Burney
  - **Best for:** Waterfall · 129-ft spring-fed falls flowing year-round — Known as the '8th Wonder of the World' (Theodore Roosevelt's phrase): Burney Falls, just outside the tiny town of Burney, California — and it's only a short walk from the parking lot. The 129-foot falls push through about 100 million gallons of water a day, and because they're mainly fed by underground springs they flow all year round. A little over an hour from Redding, which the reel suggests as home base. Note from the reel: during peak summer season, weekend visits need a reservation booked in advance (none needed after late September).
  - 🥾 AllTrails: [Trail page](https://www.alltrails.com/trail/us/california/burney-falls-loop-trail)
  - 🎬 Source: [Known as the '8th Wonder of the World' — Burney Falls](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DdttHNRPV8i/) by @wanderlusttravelcouple (#None in that reel)

Note for the merger: an existing "Yosemite Falls" place already exists in places.json, so this post is an additional source for it, not a new place. No media narrative was stored for this post (media-understanding returned a null narrative and the zeitgeist fallback's content understanding was empty), so this is based on the caption only.

### Alpine Lakes Wilderness

#### Washington

##### Hike

- **1. [Snow Lake (Alpine Lakes)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Snow+Lake+Trail+Snoqualmie+Pass+WA)** — Alpine Lakes Wilderness, Snoqualmie Pass
  - **Best for:** Hike — The Trekking Mama's 'how is this real?' lake: Snow Lake looks like it should require a plane ticket or a major expedition, but the trailhead is only about 60 minutes from Seattle. The water is an unbelievable bright alpine blue that looks photoshopped but isn't. It's popular and you'll share the trail — her tip is to keep going past the first viewpoints down toward the end of the lake, where the crowds thin out. Her go-to when someone visits Washington and needs a massive dose of alpine gorgeousness without planning a whole trip.
  - 🥾 AllTrails: [Trail page](https://www.alltrails.com/trail/us/washington/snow-lake-trail)
  - The Alpine Lakes Wilderness Snow Lake near Snoqualmie Pass — a different lake from the Snow Lake in Mount Rainier National Park, which is a separate pin. Pin is OpenStreetMap's named point for the lake.
  - 🎬 Source: [Snow Lake — the 'how is this real?' lake an hour from Seattle](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DaiuZDzPo9J/) by @the.trekking.mama (#1 in that reel)

### Alvord Desert

#### Oregon

##### Stargazing

- **2. [Alvord Desert](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Alvord+Desert+Oregon)** — Alvord Desert, Fields
  - **Best for:** Stargazing — Jess drove 9 hours to camp on the Alvord Desert playa — under some of the darkest skies in Oregon — to teach herself astrophotography. She timed the trip under an almost-full moon by accident, so she waited for moonset, set a 3:24 AM alarm, and manually shot 105 long exposures (her old Sony has no intervalometer) to stitch a four-second starlapse. Her verdict: the Alvord had been on her list a long time and was worth every bit of the drive.
  - Coordinates verified against OpenStreetMap.
  - 🎬 Source: [Alvord Desert — a 3:24 AM starlapse on the playa](https://www.instagram.com/reel/Dd19e-FTUn_/) by @jessonthewildway (#1 in that reel)

### Cape Flattery (Makah Reservation)

#### Washington

##### Viewpoint

- **3. [Cape Flattery](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Cape+Flattery+WA)** — Cape Flattery (Makah Reservation), Neah Bay
  - **Best for:** Viewpoint — The Griffins' verdict: even on a foggy day, Cape Flattery was unforgettable — the farthest northwest point of the Olympic Peninsula (and the contiguous U.S.), and one of those places that's worth going out of your way for.
  - Coordinates verified against OpenStreetMap. Cape Flattery is on the Makah Reservation; a Makah recreation pass is required to visit.
  - 🎬 Source: [Cape Flattery, Washington State](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DcdhI5UBsTA/) by @griffins.onthego (#1 in that reel)
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Cape Flattery — the final boss of PNW-maxxing](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfF6uHDSyVj/) by @giselle.on.trails
    - **Best for:** Viewpoint — Giselle calls it 'the final boss of PNW-maxxing' — the most northwestern place in the lower 48. The hike is 1.5 miles round trip with about 200 ft of gain: forest trails, boardwalks, stairs and multiple ocean viewpoints. A Makah Recreation Permit is required (online or in Neah Bay before the trailhead); open daily from dawn until 30 minutes after sunset, year-round. Her tips: it's a remote drive with spotty cell service, so download directions ahead, bring a rain jacket, and stay on the designated trails and viewing platforms.

### Mount Si Natural Resources Conservation Area

#### Washington

##### Hike

- **10. [Mount Si](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Mount+Si+Trail+North+Bend+WA)** — Mount Si Natural Resources Conservation Area, North Bend
  - **Best for:** Hike — WTA calls Mount Si the most popular hike in Washington — upwards of 100,000 hikers a year. The switchbacks start right at the trailhead; a mile and a half up you pass through Snag Flat, old-growth trees that survived fire and logging, then past a talus slope to the base of the Haystack, the true summit. 8.0 miles round trip with 3,150 ft of gain. Climbers training for Rainier haul heavy packs up here — the rule of thumb is that if you can make the top in under two hours, you're ready. A Discover Pass is required to park, and the lot fills early.
  - 🥾 AllTrails: [Trail page](https://www.alltrails.com/trail/us/washington/mount-si-trail)
  - Coordinates verified against OpenStreetMap.
  - 🎬 Source: [Mount Si — Washington's most popular hike](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfSQ-1wGjff/) by @outdoorsapp (#1 in that reel)

    - **Best for:** Remote park — His #7: visited on the same flight tour as Kobuk Valley; beautiful, remote wilderness with aerial views of braided rivers cutting through forested mountains and misty peaks, plus a ground shot of a gravel riverbed landing area.

##### Wildlife

  - **Best for:** Wildlife — Captioned 'This guy… in Glacier Bay'; tagged location Glacier Bay National Park and Preserve. On-screen text: 'Lookie what we found...' The video zooms from a turquoise bay and tree-covered coastline to a light-brown bear swimming at the tide line, hauling out onto seaweed-covered rocks, shaking off water and foraging in the intertidal zone — a park-level wildlife sighting, no specific spot named.
  - Park-level pin is Bartlett Cove, the park's headquarters and main visitor area.

    - **Best for:** Harbor seals / Wildlife — Caption names Glacier Bay NP. In a glacial fjord with turquoise water, ice chunks, and snow-capped mountains, roughly seven or eight harbor seals lie together on an ice floe and lift their heads toward the camera.

    - **Best for:** Boat tour / Wildlife — His #3 Alaska experience: the Kenai Fjords Boat Tour, featuring waterfalls on green cliffs, sea-lion colonies, sea otters, and massive tidewater glaciers calving into the sea.

### Lake Clark National Park and Preserve

#### Alaska

##### Bear viewing · Coastal brown bears on the Cook Inlet shore

  - **Best for:** Bear viewing · Coastal brown bears on the Cook Inlet shore — One of the guide's named sights: at the southern end of the park's Cook Inlet coast, where as many as twenty coastal brown bears can be visible from a single spot during the season.
  - 🎬 Source: [Save these places for Lake Clark National Park!](https://www.instagram.com/p/De0Uuigv9sW/) by @funsizewanderer (#None in that reel)

##### Bear viewing · Salmon lake in the Chigmit Mountains

- **14. [Crescent Lake](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Crescent+Lake+Lake+Clark+Alaska)** — Lake Clark National Park and Preserve, Crescent Lake
  - **Best for:** Bear viewing · Salmon lake in the Chigmit Mountains — One of the guide's named sights: a salmon lake in the heart of the Chigmit Mountains — sockeye and silver runs draw brown and black bears to the shoreline; most viewing is from boats, and visitors arrive by floatplane.
  - Pin is the Crescent Lake Ranger Station, whose coordinates NPS publishes; the lake is roadless with no developed trails.
  - 🎬 Source: [Save these places for Lake Clark National Park!](https://www.instagram.com/p/De0Uuigv9sW/) by @funsizewanderer (#None in that reel)

##### Bucket list

- **15. [Lake Clark National Park and Preserve](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=Lake+Clark+National+Park+and+Preserve+AK)** — Lake Clark National Park and Preserve, Port Alsworth
  - **Best for:** Bucket list — Ranked #2 bucket list park, with braided rivers, snow-capped mountains, glaciers and a brown bear with cubs in a meadow.
  - 🎬 Source: [My Top 5 Bucket List National Parks](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DezrbJsPMPY/) by @amorywild (#2 in that reel)
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [5 Most Expensive National Parks](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DeJr8XZO90W/) by @amorywild
    - **Best for:** Cost / Trip planning — His #3 most expensive park ($3,400 minimum), requiring a main flight plus an expensive flight tour; shown with aerial glacier views and a bear in a meadow.
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Ranking Alaska National Parks — Part 1](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DeBE2lJt_Yw/) by @amorywild
    - **Best for:** Wildlife / Scenery — His #5: praised for stunning scenery and wildlife — green meadows with brown bears including a mother with three cubs, aerial views of braided glacial rivers, and dramatic snow-covered mountain peaks.
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Wildlife / Scenic flight — Lake Clark National Park and Preserve](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DdwVrJouVO9/) by @amorywild
    - **Best for:** Aerial view / Glacier scenery — Caption names Lake Clark NP. From a small aircraft, the video pans across rugged snow-covered peaks and extensive glaciers; on-screen text reads, 'Hoping to see a view this beautiful again one day...'
  - 🎬 Also recommended by: [Save these places for Lake Clark National Park!](https://www.instagram.com/p/De0Uuigv9sW/) by @funsizewanderer
    - **Best for:** National park guide — A full park guide: best time to visit is July–September. Sights include Lake Clark itself, Proenneke Cabin at Twin Lakes, Turquoise Lake, Port Alsworth, Chinitna Bay, Crescent Lake, Redoubt and Iliamna volcanoes, and Telaquana Lake; top hikes are Beaver Pond Loop (3.5 mi easy), Teetering Rock (1.8), Tanalian Falls (4.3), Kontrashibuna Lake (4.3) and Tanalian Mountain (9.6 hard). The guide's key tip: the park has no road access — arrive by air taxi or floatplane from Anchorage or Homer, and build in buffer days because pass weather can ground bush planes.

  - What the reel recommends: Recommends Canyon Overlook Trail as one of Zion's best views at the end of a 1-mile trail: biggest payoff for least effort in Zion, ~160 ft gain, 1-2 hours, just east of the Zion-Mount Carmel Tunnel, incredible at sunrise or sunset, views over Pine Creek Canyon. This place was already on the map from the @amorywild sunrise reel, so it was added as a second source on that pin instead of a duplicate pin.
- **[Canyon Overlook Trail — Epic View Without Angels Landing](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfP7jlPPlAI/)** — @beesforbeau · 1 places · Theme: Short hike with an epic view, Angels Landing alternative
  - What the reel recommends: Recommends Canyon Overlook Trail as an epic-view alternative to Angels Landing without the chains, climb or permit: about 1 mile round trip, allow about an hour, just east of the Zion-Mount Carmel Tunnel, with sandstone formations, rock alcoves and exposed sections. Parking is very limited, arrive early. Already on the map from two earlier reels, so added as a third source on that pin.

Ranked #2, a 17-mile route with about 4,500 feet of gain done in June with snow at the top, which he calls one of the most beautiful hikes he has done despite loose rock. Ranked #1, an 8-mile hike with about 4,500 feet of gain and turquoise lake views. He says he was unprepared on his first attempt and later returned better prepared.

- **[Mt. Monadnock in New Hampshire](https://www.instagram.com/reel/De93H7Iyu6a/)** — @calvin_on_the_internet · 1 places · Theme: New Hampshire hikes
  - What the reel recommends: Mount Monadnock in Jaffrey, New Hampshire — the iconic 3,165-ft solitary peak in Monadnock State Park, climbed via the White Dot Trail to a bare summit with six-state views on clear days.
- **[Known as the '8th Wonder of the World' — Burney Falls](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DdttHNRPV8i/)** — @wanderlusttravelcouple · 1 places · Theme: California waterfalls
  - What the reel recommends: Burney Falls in McArthur-Burney Falls Memorial State Park, California — a 129-ft spring-fed waterfall flowing ~100M gallons a day, year-round, a short walk from parking; summer weekends need reservations.
- **[An underground waterfall inside Lookout Mountain: Ruby Falls](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfZ5L1ivynd/)** — @wanderlusttravelcouple · 1 places · Theme: Tennessee waterfalls
  - What the reel recommends: Ruby Falls in Chattanooga, Tennessee — the underground waterfall deep inside Lookout Mountain, reached by elevator and a guided cave walk; added as a second source on the existing pin.
- **[12 bucket-list hikes NOT in national parks](https://www.instagram.com/reel/DfYrTw7t0-W/)** — @theadventurepostcard · 12 places · Theme: Bucket-list hikes outside national parks
  - What the reel recommends: A ranked countdown of 12 bucket-list hikes that are NOT in national parks, from The Wave (#1) to Cathedral Rock (#12): The Wave, Colchuck Lake, Kasha-Katuwe Tent Rocks, Hanging Lake, Ice Lake Basin, Bisti Wings, Blue Lakes, Sawtooth Lake, Awa'awapuhi, Cathedral Spires, Havasu Falls and Cathedral Rock.
- **[Sunrise hike at Guadalupe Peak, the highest point in Texas](https://www.instagram.com/reel/Deb8XEtRmcz/)** — @serg.goes · 1 places · Theme: Texas hikes

  - What the reel recommends: A bucket-list montage of 13 USA landscapes: Napali Coast, Katmai bears, Sequoia, White Mountains fall lake, Maroon Bells, Channel Islands, Voyageurs, Grand Staircase-Escalante arch, Smokies ridges, Rainier waterfall, a San Juan Mountains alpine lake (attached to Ice Lake Basin), Monument Valley, and the North Cascades.

## How this page is updated {#how-this-page-is-updated}

Send Muse an Instagram reel link in chat. Muse reads the caption and the video itself, extracts every named location, verifies and geocodes each one, assigns its region and state, records what the reel recommended it for and a link to the reel, then regenerates this list and the map from `places.json` and pushes the update to GitHub Pages. Duplicates (same name + state) are not added twice.
